### `flutter3d_app`

- <a id="app-BinaryStorage"></a>**`BinaryStorage`** (by hand). `BinaryStorage` is an `abstract base class`: an implementation is written `extends BinaryStorage`. Its members are unchanged.
- <a id="f2-app-Decal3D-colours"></a>**`Decal3D`** (by hand). `color` and `emissive` are linear `LinearColor`s; the old `color` drew as sRGB, so `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a)` keeps the picture.
- <a id="f1-rename-app-DocumentText"></a>**`DocumentText`** (`dart fix`). `DocumentText` is `LevelDocumentText`. The function `LevelLoader` reads a level's text through is `LevelDocumentText`; `DocumentText` is the editor's writer of documents in `flutter3d_editor_core`.
- <a id="app-FileStorage"></a>**`FileStorage`** (by hand). `FileStorage.read`, `write` and `remove` are asynchronous, as `Storage` is: await them, and catch `StorageException` from `write` where `false` was checked.
- <a id="f2-app-Flutter3dEngine-device"></a>**`Flutter3dEngine`** (nothing to do). `device` and `renderer` are getters where they were final fields, since a device loss may replace either; every read compiles as it did.
- <a id="c-app-Issue"></a>**`Issue`** (`dart fix`). `Issue` is imported from `package:flutter3d_foundation/flutter3d_foundation.dart`. The one issue type every package reports through is the foundation's; `flutter3d_app` keeps `IssueLog` and `printIssue` and no longer re-exports the type.
- <a id="c-app-IssueSink"></a>**`IssueSink`** (`dart fix`). `IssueSink` is imported from `package:flutter3d_foundation/flutter3d_foundation.dart`. `IssueSink` is the foundation's, beside `Issue`.
- <a id="f4-ui-LevelLoadFailed-startOverLabel"></a>**`LevelLoadFailed`** (nothing to do). Nullable, as `LoadingScreen.message`: null is `Flutter3dAppLocalizations.startOver` in the reader's language.
- <a id="app-LevelLoader"></a>**`LevelLoader`** (nothing to do). Only the engine's material type in its signature changed: `Material` is `RenderMaterial` (`core-Material-RenderMaterial` renames it).
- <a id="f2-app-Light3D-color"></a>**`Light3D.color`** (by hand). `color` is a linear `LinearColor`: `Vector3(r, g, b)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="f4-ui-status-screen-words"></a>**`LoadingScreen`** (nothing to do). `LoadingScreen.message` and `LevelLoadFailed.startOverLabel` are nullable now: left out, they are the reader's language from `Flutter3dAppLocalizations`, English when the app installs no delegate. A caller that passes a string still says its own words; only code reading the field as a non-null `String` sees the change.
- <a id="f2-app-colour-widgets"></a>**`Material3D`** (by hand). `Material3D.baseColor` and `emissive` are linear `LinearColor`s (`baseColor: LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b)` for a colour picked by eye, which is what the old `Vector4` drew as), and `emissiveStrength` is nits: multiply a pre-1.0 number by `Photometric.legacyNits`.
- <a id="f2-app-Mirror3D-tint"></a>**`Mirror3D.tint`** (by hand). `tint` is a linear `LinearColor`: `Vector3(r, g, b)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="f2-app-Scene3D-clearColor"></a>**`Scene3D.clearColor`** (by hand). `Scene3D(clearColor:)` takes a `LinearColor`, in linear light, and encodes it to sRGB for the view. The `Vector4` it took was handed to the view as sRGB whatever its doc said, so to keep the picture write the old numbers as `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a)`.
- <a id="f2-app-Scene3DController"></a>**`Scene3DController`** (nothing to do). `device`, `renderer` and `scene` are getters where they were fields, reading through the new `engine`; every read compiles as it did.
- <a id="app-SceneSurface"></a>**`SceneSurface`** (by hand). `SceneSurface` is a `StatefulWidget` now, so it can own the frame callback it takes as `onFrame`. Building one is unchanged; a class that extended it and overrode `build` has to wrap a `SceneSurface` in a widget of its own instead.
- <a id="app-Storage"></a>**`Storage`** (by hand). `Storage` is asynchronous and an `abstract base class`. `read` answers `Future<String?>`, `write` a `Future<void>` that throws a `StorageException` when the platform refused, and `remove` a `Future<void>`: await them, and catch `StorageException` where a refused write used to be `false`. An implementation is written `extends Storage` with `async` overrides; a test can use `MemoryStorage` instead of its own.
- <a id="app-TerrainTiles"></a>**`TerrainTiles`** (nothing to do). Only the engine's material type in its signature changed: `Material` is `RenderMaterial` (`core-Material-RenderMaterial` renames it).
- <a id="4a-app-TerrainTiles-TerrainTiles"></a>**`TerrainTiles.TerrainTiles`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-app-TerrainTiles-metresPerTexture"></a>**`TerrainTiles.metresPerTexture`** (`dart fix`). `TerrainTiles.metresPerTexture` is `metersPerTexture`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-app-WidgetSurface-WidgetSurface"></a>**`WidgetSurface.WidgetSurface`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="f2-app-defaultDevices"></a>**`defaultDevices`** (by hand). There is no process-wide device registry. An engine owns one (`Flutter3dEngine.devices`, or `Flutter3dView(devices: ...)`), and an application that wants a backend or presenter of its own keeps a registry from `platformDevices()` and passes it: `openDevice(width: w, height: h, registry: mine)`. A device `openDevice` opened remembers its registry, so `presentFrame` finds the presenter without being handed it again. A test that put a fake in front of the real backends adds it to that registry instead.
- <a id="4a-app-kFixedResolution"></a>**`kFixedResolution`** (`dart fix`). `kFixedResolution` is `fixedResolution`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="app-package-flutter3d_editor_core-flutter3d_editor_core-dart"></a>**`package:flutter3d_editor_core/flutter3d_editor_core.dart`** (`dart fix`). Names re-exported from `package:flutter3d_editor_core/flutter3d_editor_core.dart` are imported from `package:flutter3d_level_scene/flutter3d_level_scene.dart`. `flutter3d_app` re-exported `LevelBatching` and `meshDataOf` from the editor's core; it depends on neither the editor nor a re-export now. Import `package:flutter3d_level_scene/flutter3d_level_scene.dart` for them; `dart fix` adds the import, and `migrate` the dependency.

### `flutter3d_audio`

- <a id="f4-audio-AudioIssue"></a>**`AudioIssue`** (`dart fix`). `AudioIssue` is `Issue`. `AudioIssue` is `Issue`, the type `flutter3d_app` reports in, now declared in `flutter3d_foundation` and exported by `flutter3d_audio` and `flutter3d_app` alike, with the same `message`.
- <a id="f4-audio-AudioIssueSink"></a>**`AudioIssueSink`** (`dart fix`). `AudioIssueSink` is `IssueSink`. `AudioIssueSink` is `IssueSink`, `void Function(Issue issue)`.
- <a id="f4-audio-SoLoudBackend"></a>**`SoLoudBackend`** (nothing to do). `onIssue` is an `IssueSink` where it was an `AudioIssueSink`; the two renames above carry every caller, and a sink written as a closure needs no change.
- <a id="f4-audio-Speakers"></a>**`Speakers`** (by hand). `Speakers.backend` is an `AudioBackend`, not a `SoLoudBackend`. Declare what you read it into as `AudioBackend`; `speakers.pause()`, `resume()` and `dispose()` are on `Speakers` itself. Code that needs the SoLoud class's own members (`failedAssets`) casts with `as SoLoudBackend`.
- <a id="f4-audio-openSpeakers"></a>**`openSpeakers`** (by hand). `openSpeakers` answers `Future<Speakers>` and throws `AudioDeviceException` when no audio device opens, where it answered null. Wrap the call in `try { ... } on AudioDeviceException { ... }` and play silent in the `on` branch; drop the `== null` check, and declare what you read it into as `Speakers`. `onIssue` takes an `IssueSink`.
- <a id="audio-package-flutter3d_audio-testing-dart"></a>**`package:flutter3d_audio/testing.dart`** (by hand). `soundTableIn` was this repository's check of its demo games' sound banks against their source, not engine API, and is no longer published. Copy it into your own test helpers: it reads the file, collects the names after `static const SoundDef` and the names inside `static final SoundBank all = SoundBank(<SoundDef>[…])`, and returns both sets.

### `flutter3d_audio_core`

- <a id="audio_core-AudioBackend"></a>**`AudioBackend`** (by hand). `AudioBackend` is an `abstract base class`: write `extends AudioBackend` where you wrote `implements AudioBackend`, and mark the class `final`, `base` or `sealed`. The five voice members are still yours to write. `open`, `pause`, `resume` and `dispose` have bodies that do nothing; an `open` or `dispose` your backend already had now overrides them (add `@override`). `MixingBackend` and `DirectionalBackend` no longer extend it, so a backend that offers them writes `extends AudioBackend implements MixingBackend`.
- <a id="audio_core-AudioScene"></a>**`AudioScene`** (nothing to do). `emitters` and `play` return `AudioEmitter`, the new name of `SoundEmitter`; the old name is a typedef of the new one, so code that holds the result as a `SoundEmitter` still compiles. `play` gained the optional `bus` and `follow`.
- <a id="4a-audio_core-LoopBand-LoopBand"></a>**`LoopBand.LoopBand`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-audio_core-LoopBand-centre"></a>**`LoopBand.centre`** (`dart fix`). `LoopBand.centre` is `center`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-audio_core-SilentVoice-alive"></a>**`SilentVoice.alive`** (`dart fix`). `SilentVoice.alive` is `isAlive`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="audio_core-SoundEmitter"></a>**`SoundEmitter`** (`dart fix`). `SoundEmitter` is `AudioEmitter`. `SoundEmitter` is `AudioEmitter` in the 1.0 audio model, with the same members. The old name is not kept as a typedef; `dart fix` renames every use.

### `flutter3d_build`

- <a id="build-six-way-bake-import"></a>**`package:flutter3d_build/src/six_way_bake.dart`** (`migrate`). `package:flutter3d_build/src/six_way_bake.dart` is `package:flutter3d_particles/flutter3d_particles.dart`. The six-way smoke bake moved to `flutter3d_particles`, which owns the particle look it feeds; its public names are on the package's main library.
- <a id="build-format-exceptions"></a>**`ConvertCommandOptions`** (by hand). The readers in flutter3d_build throw their own leaf of `Flutter3dFormatException` instead of `dart:core`'s `FormatException`: `ConvertUsageException` (`ConvertCommandSettings.parse`), `SourceFormatException` (a PLY, USD, Godot or Unity text file, XML, a ZIP, or a file `decodeModelFile` has no reader for) and `MigrationTableException` (a migration table). An `on FormatException` around one of them no longer catches: catch the leaf, or `Flutter3dFormatException` for all of them.
- <a id="4a-build-ConvertOptions"></a>**`ConvertOptions`** (`dart fix`). `ConvertOptions` is `ConvertSettings`. Settings classes have one suffix in 1.0, Settings (a HAL type's is Descriptor), and each is final, const, with a copyWith that covers every field. The class is the same under the new name.
- <a id="build-ManifestFormatException"></a>**`ManifestFormatException`** (nothing to do). `ManifestFormatException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on ManifestFormatException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="build-SixWayField"></a>**`SixWayField`** (`dart fix`). `SixWayField` is imported from `package:flutter3d_particles/flutter3d_particles.dart`.
- <a id="build-SixWaySheet"></a>**`SixWaySheet`** (`dart fix`). `SixWaySheet` is imported from `package:flutter3d_particles/flutter3d_particles.dart`.
- <a id="f3-build-TextureFamily-parse"></a>**`TextureFamily.parse`** (by hand). `TextureFamily.parse` throws a `ConvertUsageException` naming the families there are, instead of answering null for a word it does not know. A caller that had a fallback asks `TextureFamily.tryParse`, which answers null as `parse` did: `TextureFamily.tryParse(text) ?? fallback`.
- <a id="build-bakeSixWay"></a>**`bakeSixWay`** (`dart fix`). `bakeSixWay` is imported from `package:flutter3d_particles/flutter3d_particles.dart`.
- <a id="build-decodeModelFile-exception"></a>**`decodeModelFile`** (by hand). A file no reader claims, or one that is already a `.f3d`, throws `SourceFormatException` (a `Flutter3dFormatException`) instead of `dart:core`'s `FormatException`. Catch that, or the family.
- <a id="build-convert-confined"></a>**`fileUriResolverFor`** (by hand). A converted document reads only files inside its own directory (for `convertFiles`, inside the upload): a buffer, texture, `mtllib` or USD reference that climbs out with `..` or is absolute is refused. Move the files it names under the model's directory, or pass `root:`.
- <a id="build-kAssetPipelineVersion"></a>**`kAssetPipelineVersion`** (nothing to do). Only the value of the constant moved; code that reads `assetPipelineVersion` gets the new one. It is a version or a layout size the engine stamps and checks itself.
- <a id="4a-build-kAssetPipelineVersion"></a>**`kAssetPipelineVersion`** (`dart fix`). `kAssetPipelineVersion` is `assetPipelineVersion`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix.
- <a id="4a-build-kDefaultChunkThreshold"></a>**`kDefaultChunkThreshold`** (`dart fix`). `kDefaultChunkThreshold` is `defaultChunkThreshold`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="build-kFlutter3dBuildVersionConstraint"></a>**`kFlutter3dBuildVersionConstraint`** (nothing to do). Only the value of the constant moved; code that reads `flutter3dBuildVersionConstraint` gets the new one. It is a version the engine stamps and checks itself.
- <a id="4a-build-kFlutter3dBuildVersionConstraint"></a>**`kFlutter3dBuildVersionConstraint`** (`dart fix`). `kFlutter3dBuildVersionConstraint` is `flutter3dBuildVersionConstraint`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix.
- <a id="4a-build-kGitignoreEntry"></a>**`kGitignoreEntry`** (`dart fix`). `kGitignoreEntry` is `gitignoreEntry`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-build-kHookBuildContent"></a>**`kHookBuildContent`** (`dart fix`). `kHookBuildContent` is `hookBuildContent`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="c-build-core-library"></a>**`package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`** (`dart fix`). Names re-exported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart` are imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`. `DeviceClass` and `deviceClassPath` are the core's, and `flutter3d_build` no longer re-exports them.
- <a id="f3-build-parseLodRatios"></a>**`parseLodRatios`** (by hand). `parseLodRatios` answers a non-null list and throws a `ConvertUsageException` naming the entry that is not a ratio between 0 and 1, instead of answering null. Drop the `?? …` after the call, and catch `ConvertUsageException` where the null was handled.
- <a id="4a-build-recognisedExtensions"></a>**`recognisedExtensions`** (`dart fix`). `recognisedExtensions` is `recognizedExtensions`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="build-smokePuff"></a>**`smokePuff`** (`dart fix`). `smokePuff` is imported from `package:flutter3d_particles/flutter3d_particles.dart`.
- <a id="4a-build-splitLargeMeshes"></a>**`splitLargeMeshes`** (nothing to do). Only a type its declaration names was renamed; that rename's own fix carries every use.

### `flutter3d_conformance`

- <a id="conformance-ConformanceDeclined"></a>**`ConformanceDeclined`** (`dart fix`). `ConformanceDeclined` is `ConformanceDeclinedException`. `ConformanceDeclined` is `ConformanceDeclinedException` (decision H: a type that is thrown is named `*Exception`), with the same members. It now extends `CapabilityException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly, so `on Exception` still catches it; `dart fix` renames every use.
- <a id="conformance-ConformanceFailure"></a>**`ConformanceFailure`** (`dart fix`). `ConformanceFailure` is `ConformanceFailureException`. `ConformanceFailure` is `ConformanceFailureException` (decision H: a type that is thrown is named `*Exception`), with the same members. It now extends `CapabilityException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly, so `on Exception` still catches it; `dart fix` renames every use.
- <a id="f1-conformance-PluginHarness-world"></a>**`PluginHarness`** (by hand). `PluginHarness` has no `capture`, `restore` or `digest`: the world the plugin acts on is the loop's. In `setUp`, put the application's state in `loop.world`, or add a part for it — `loop.snapshots.add(SnapshotPart.of(id: 'my.world', capture: world.save, restore: (data, _) => world.load(data)))` — and the checks capture, restore and digest it through `EngineLoop.snapshots`, the path a rollback takes. `hasWorld` is gone: a loop whose snapshots hold nothing is a run with no world.

### `flutter3d_core`

- <a id="internal-flutter3d_core"></a>**6 names were the package's own** (gone internal): they are not exported since 1.0.0-rc.1, and the import of `flutter3d_core` that brought them gets one diagnostic for all of them. <a id="core-DrawItem"></a>`DrawItem`, <a id="core-FramePassState"></a>`FramePassState`, <a id="core-LightBuffer"></a>`LightBuffer`, <a id="core-RenderList"></a>`RenderList`, <a id="core-ShaderLightType"></a>`ShaderLightType`, <a id="core-package-flutter3d_core-parity_scene-dart"></a>`package:flutter3d_core/parity_scene.dart`. A node or contributor uses `FrameContext.noteDraw` and `FrameContext.invalidatePipeline`. The limits are `LightNode.maxLights` and `LightNode.maxExtraLights`, `LightBuffer.reaches(light, channels)` is `light.reaches(channels)`, and a scene's overflow is `scene.overflowingLights`. A frame says what it drew in `FrameResult`. Use `LightType`. A backend's own tests import it from `package:flutter3d_core/src/engine/render/parity_scene.dart`.
- <a id="core-AbsolutePixels"></a>**`AbsolutePixels`** (`dart fix`). `AbsolutePixels` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="f3-core-AnimationPointer-parse"></a>**`AnimationPointer.parse`** (by hand). `AnimationPointer.parse` throws an `AnimationPointerFormatException` for a pointer this engine does not animate, instead of answering null. Code that checked for null (a loader meeting a file's pointer) calls `AnimationPointer.tryParse`, which answers null as `parse` did.
- <a id="core-AnimationPointerSink"></a>**`AnimationPointerSink`** (`migrate`). `AnimationPointerSink` is a base mixin class: `implements AnimationPointerSink` becomes `with AnimationPointerSink`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="core-AnimationTarget"></a>**`AnimationTarget`** (`migrate`). `AnimationTarget` is a base mixin class: `implements AnimationTarget` becomes `with AnimationTarget`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="core-Atmosphere"></a>**`Atmosphere`** (by hand). Its colours are `LinearColor`s (`sky: const LinearColor(0.4, 0.6, 0.9)`, or `vector.toLinearColor()`), it is `const`, and `sunIntensity` is lux: multiply a pre-1.0 value by `Photometric.legacyUnit`.
- <a id="f2-core-Atmosphere-ambientIntensity"></a>**`Atmosphere.ambientIntensity`** (by hand). `Atmosphere.ambientIntensity` is lux, as `sunIntensity` already was: multiply a pre-1.0 number by `Photometric.legacyUnit`.
- <a id="core-Atmosphere-applyTo-clearColor"></a>**`Atmosphere.applyTo`** (`dart fix`). `applyTo(scene, clearColor: v)` is `applyTo(scene, clearColorSrgb: v)`, the view's `RenderView.clearColorSrgb`. The sky is linear and is now encoded into that sRGB slot, where it was written as it was, so a clear colour set from an atmosphere is lighter than before: the colour the sky was meant to be.
- <a id="core-BoolCondition"></a>**`BoolCondition`** (by hand). `AnimationCondition` has a new case, `BoolCondition`: a `switch` over a `AnimationCondition` that names every case needs one for it, or a wildcard.
- <a id="rc-core-CellGrid-mesh"></a>**`CellGrid.mesh`** (by hand). `mesh(color:)` takes a linear `LinearColor`: `Vector4(r, g, b, a)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b, a)`.
- <a id="4a-core-CellGrid-mesh"></a>**`CellGrid.mesh`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="f3-core-CheckedModelWriter-base"></a>**`CheckedModelWriter`** (by hand). `CheckedModelWriter` is an `abstract base class` that extends `ModelWriter`. A writer that wrote `implements CheckedModelWriter` now writes `extends CheckedModelWriter` and is `final` or `base` itself; its members stay as they were.
- <a id="core-ClusterDraws"></a>**`ClusterDraws`** (`dart fix`). `ClusterDraws` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-CompareCondition"></a>**`CompareCondition`** (by hand). `AnimationCondition` has a new case, `CompareCondition`: a `switch` over a `AnimationCondition` that names every case needs one for it, or a wildcard.
- <a id="core-ContributorFrame"></a>**`ContributorFrame`** (by hand). A contributor counts its draw with `frame.noteDraw()` and says it bound a pipeline of its own with `frame.invalidatePipeline()`; the counters behind them are the renderer's. `ContributorFrame` is a `FrameContext` now, and also says the view's projection, its jitter, the time and the scene's origin.
- <a id="core-ContributorLights"></a>**`ContributorLights`** (`migrate`). `ContributorLights` is a base mixin class: `implements ContributorLights` becomes `with ContributorLights`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="4a-core-ContributorLights-bind"></a>**`ContributorLights.bind`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="core-ContributorRegistry"></a>**`ContributorRegistry`** (by hand). `Renderer.contributors` and `addContributor` moved to the one registry: `renderer.renderSteps.addContributor(c)`, `removeContributor(c)`, and `renderSteps.contributors` to list them.
- <a id="rc-core-DebugColors"></a>**`DebugColors`** (by hand). The overlay colours are linear `LinearColor`s, `static final` where they were getters making a `Vector4`; `DebugDraw` encodes them back to the same display colours. Pass them on as they are; a read of `.x` is `.r`, in linear light.
- <a id="rc-core-DebugDraw-colours"></a>**`DebugDraw`** (by hand). A line's colour is a linear `LinearColor`, which the overlay encodes for the display; the `Vector4` was written to the screen as it was, an sRGB colour. The same line is `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a)` of the old numbers: `addLine(a, b, Vector4(1, 0.5, 0, 1))` is `addLine(a, b, LinearColor.fromSrgb(1, 0.5, 0))`. The points are in scene space; a place in the world goes in through `addWorldLine`.
- <a id="rc-core-DebugDrawGizmos-colours"></a>**`DebugDrawGizmos`** (by hand). Every gizmo's colour (`addBox`, `addTransformedBox`, `addFrustum`, `addSphere`, `addBoneOctahedron`, `addJointCross`, `addNormals`, `addSkeletonOverlay`) is a linear `LinearColor`; see `rc-core-DebugDraw-colours` for why the old numbers go through `LinearColor.fromSrgb`.
- <a id="4a-core-DebugDrawGizmos-addSphere"></a>**`DebugDrawGizmos.addSphere`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-DebugDrawGizmos-buildForScene"></a>**`DebugDrawGizmos.buildForScene`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-DebugDrawOptions"></a>**`DebugDrawOptions`** (`dart fix`). `DebugDrawOptions` is `DebugDrawSettings`. Settings classes have one suffix in 1.0, Settings (a HAL type's is Descriptor), and each is final, const, with a copyWith that covers every field. The class is the same under the new name.
- <a id="f2-core-DecalNode-colours"></a>**`DecalNode`** (by hand). `DecalNode.color` is a linear `LinearColor` with the opacity in its alpha (it was an sRGB `Vector4`: `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a)` keeps the picture), and `emissive` a linear `LinearColor`. Both are assigned whole: `decal.color.w = o` is `decal.color = decal.color.withAlpha(o)`.
- <a id="core-DeviceClassMemory"></a>**`DeviceClassMemory`** (by hand). An `abstract base class` since 1.0: write `extends DeviceClassMemory` where you wrote `implements DeviceClassMemory`, and mark the class `final`, `base` or `sealed`.
- <a id="core-DracoException"></a>**`DracoException`** (nothing to do). `DracoException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on DracoException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="core-DrawableGeometry"></a>**`DrawableGeometry`** (by hand). `DrawableGeometry` is an `abstract base class` that mixes in `MeshGeometry`. Code that draws one is unchanged. A geometry of your own that implemented it now `extends DrawableGeometry` (and is `final` or `base` itself), and keeps the members it wrote.
- <a id="4a-core-EffectiveAntiAliasing-none"></a>**`EffectiveAntiAliasing.none`** (`dart fix`). `EffectiveAntiAliasing.none` is `isNone`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="f2-core-EnvironmentMap-fromEncoded"></a>**`EnvironmentMap.fromEncoded`** (by hand). Completes with the environment, not a nullable one: bytes neither reader takes throw a `PanoramaFormatException`, and the rest as `fromPanorama`.
- <a id="f2-core-EnvironmentMap-fromPanorama"></a>**`EnvironmentMap.fromPanorama`** (by hand). Returns the environment, not a nullable one: a device without cubes throws `UnsupportedCapability` (ask `EnvironmentMap.isSupportedOn`), and pixels that are not `width × height` RGBA8 an `ArgumentError`.
- <a id="f2-core-EnvironmentMap-fromSky"></a>**`EnvironmentMap.fromSky`** (by hand). Returns the environment, not a nullable one: a device without cubes throws `UnsupportedCapability` and a sky that is off an `ArgumentError`. Ask `EnvironmentMap.isSupportedOn(device)` (and `sky.enabled`) first where the null meant "keep the flat ambient".
- <a id="core-F3dFormatException"></a>**`F3dFormatException`** (nothing to do). `F3dFormatException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on F3dFormatException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="core-FabrikIk"></a>**`FabrikIk`** (nothing to do). Only the animation pose in its signature changed: `Pose` is `AnimationPose` (`core-Pose-AnimationPose` renames it).
- <a id="4a-core-FieldPass-spec"></a>**`FieldPass.spec`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="f2-core-settings-colours"></a>**`FogSettings`** (by hand). The colours of `FogSettings`, `LightShaftSettings`, `VolumetricFogSettings` (`color`, `ambient`) and `XraySettings` are linear `LinearColor`s, so the settings can be `const`: `color: Vector3(r, g, b)` is `color: LinearColor(r, g, b)`, or `vector.toLinearColor()`.
- <a id="core-FootPlantGoal"></a>**`FootPlantGoal`** (by hand). `AnimationGoal` has a new case, `FootPlantGoal`: a `switch` over a `AnimationGoal` that names every case needs one for it, or a wildcard.
- <a id="core-FrameCapture-parse"></a>**`FrameCapture`** (by hand). `FrameCapture.fromJson` and `FrameCapture.parse` return a capture or throw a `FrameCaptureFormatException` with the reason (another format, a newer version, no size) instead of returning null; catch it where a file may not be a capture. The constructor is no longer `const` (`FrameCapture` is a `FormatDocument` now). `fileFormat` is `f3d.frameCapture` and `fileVersion` 2; files written as `flutter3d.frameCapture` version 1 still read.
- <a id="core-FrameCaptureBuilder"></a>**`FrameCaptureBuilder`** (`dart fix`). `FrameCaptureBuilder` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="4a-core-FrameGraphNode-supported"></a>**`FrameGraphNode.supported`** (`dart fix`). `FrameGraphNode.supported` is `isSupported`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="core-FrameHistory"></a>**`FrameHistory`** (by hand). The last view-projection is kept per `RenderView` since 1.0, not per camera, so two views of one camera each keep their own: `viewProjection(view)`.
- <a id="f2-core-FrameHistory-per-view"></a>**`FrameHistory`** (by hand). The history is kept per view now, so views drawn by separate `Renderer.render` calls each have their own past. `of(node)` is `of(node, view)` and `moved(node)` is `moved(node, view)`: pass the `RenderView` whose last frame you mean, the one you hand to `render`.
- <a id="core-FramePhase"></a>**`FramePhase`** (by hand). The two phases were two anchors. Add a node with `renderer.renderSteps.addNode(node, at: RenderAnchor.afterScene)` for `FramePhase.overlay` and `at: RenderAnchor.beforePresent` for `FramePhase.present`; a node that knows where it belongs overrides `RenderNode.defaultAnchor` and is added with no `at`.
- <a id="4a-core-FrameResourceIds-hdrColour"></a>**`FrameResourceIds.hdrColour`** (`dart fix`). `FrameResourceIds.hdrColour` is `hdrColor`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-FrameResourceIds-sceneColour"></a>**`FrameResourceIds.sceneColour`** (`dart fix`). `FrameResourceIds.sceneColour` is `sceneColor`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-FrameResources-transient"></a>**`FrameResources.transient`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="core-FrameTextureSource"></a>**`FrameTextureSource`** (`migrate`). `FrameTextureSource` is a base mixin class: `implements FrameTextureSource` becomes `with FrameTextureSource`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="4a-core-FreeLook-metresPerSecond"></a>**`FreeLook.metresPerSecond`** (`dart fix`). `FreeLook.metresPerSecond` is `metersPerSecond`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-FullscreenDraw-FullscreenDraw"></a>**`FullscreenDraw.FullscreenDraw`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-FullscreenDraw-sampler"></a>**`FullscreenDraw.sampler`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-FullscreenDraw-samplerFor"></a>**`FullscreenDraw.samplerFor`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-FullscreenDraw-samplers"></a>**`FullscreenDraw.samplers`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="core-FullscreenEffect"></a>**`FullscreenEffect`** (by hand). `preferredPhase` is `defaultAnchor`. An effect no longer registers itself anywhere: add it with `renderer.renderSteps.addNode(effect)`, which places it at its own anchor.
- <a id="4a-core-GltfWriter-usedGeometryQuantization"></a>**`GltfWriter.usedGeometryQuantization`** (`dart fix`). `GltfWriter.usedGeometryQuantization` is `didQuantizeGeometry`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-GltfWriter-usedVertexCacheReordering"></a>**`GltfWriter.usedVertexCacheReordering`** (`dart fix`). `GltfWriter.usedVertexCacheReordering` is `didReorderVertexCache`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="core-HdrFormatException"></a>**`HdrFormatException`** (nothing to do). `HdrFormatException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on HdrFormatException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="f2-core-HighContrastSettings-outlineColor"></a>**`HighContrastSettings`** (by hand). `HighContrastSettings.outlineColor` is a linear `LinearColor`, encoded to the display where the line is drawn: a display colour is `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="core-ImmediateTextureSource"></a>**`ImmediateTextureSource`** (`dart fix`). `ImmediateTextureSource` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="4a-core-ImpostorNode-ImpostorNode"></a>**`ImpostorNode.ImpostorNode`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-ImpostorNode-ImpostorNode-withCard"></a>**`ImpostorNode.ImpostorNode.withCard`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-ImpostorNode-centre"></a>**`ImpostorNode.centre`** (`dart fix`). `ImpostorNode.centre` is `center`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="core-InMemoryDeviceClassMemory"></a>**`InMemoryDeviceClassMemory`** (`dart fix`). `InMemoryDeviceClassMemory` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="4a-core-InstanceHandle-live"></a>**`InstanceHandle.live`** (`dart fix`). `InstanceHandle.live` is `isLive`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="f2-core-InstanceHandle-setColor"></a>**`InstanceHandle.setColor`** (by hand). Takes a linear `LinearColor`; `vector.toLinearColor()` converts.
- <a id="core-InstancedMeshNode"></a>**`InstancedMeshNode`** (nothing to do). Only the value of the constant moved; code that reads `InstancedMeshNode` gets the new one. It is a version or a layout size the engine stamps and checks itself.
- <a id="f2-core-InstancedMeshNode-colours"></a>**`InstancedMeshNode`** (by hand). An instance's colour is a linear `LinearColor`: `addInstance(m, color: Vector4(r, g, b, a))` is `addInstance(m, color: LinearColor(r, g, b, a))`, and `InstanceHandle.setColor` takes one too. `data` stays a `Vector4`.
- <a id="4a-core-IrradianceField-writeIrradiance"></a>**`IrradianceField.writeIrradiance`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-IrradianceField-writeIrradianceTexel"></a>**`IrradianceField.writeIrradianceTexel`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="core-Ktx2FormatException"></a>**`Ktx2FormatException`** (nothing to do). `Ktx2FormatException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on Ktx2FormatException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="core-LightNode"></a>**`LightNode`** (by hand). `intensity` is in photometric units since 1.0: lux for a directional light, candela for a point, spot or area light. Multiply a pre-1.0 literal by `Photometric.legacyUnit` (about 5 790.6) and the picture is the picture it was; or write the light's real lux or candela, or make it with `LightNode.lumens`. `color` is a `LinearColor`: `color: const LinearColor(1, 0.8, 0.6)`, `light.color = vector.toLinearColor()` where it was `light.color.setValues(...)`.
- <a id="f2-core-LightNode-maxExtraLights"></a>**`LightNode.maxExtraLights`** (nothing to do). A static getter now, with the same value (24), for the reason `maxLights` is one.
- <a id="f2-core-LightNode-maxLights"></a>**`LightNode.maxLights`** (nothing to do). A static getter now, with the same value (8). Every read still compiles; only a `const` expression that used it does not, and it takes the number itself or a non-const variable.
- <a id="f2-core-LightShaftSettings-color"></a>**`LightShaftSettings`** (by hand). `color` is a linear `LinearColor`; see `f2-core-settings-colours`.
- <a id="core-LightType"></a>**`LightType`** (by hand). An open set since 1.0, so a light type a later minor adds does not break your code: a `switch` over it needs a `_ =>` case. `values`, `name` and `index` are where they were; `LightType.byName` reads a name back.
- <a id="f2-core-LightType-index"></a>**`LightType.index`** (nothing to do). A getter where it was a field, with the same values; `LightType` gained `LightType.custom` and `base`, so the set is open.
- <a id="f2-core-LineStripNode-color"></a>**`LineStripNode`** (by hand). `LineStripNode(color:)` and `color` are a linear `LinearColor`.
- <a id="4a-core-LineStripNode-LineStripNode"></a>**`LineStripNode.LineStripNode`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-LineStripNode-colour"></a>**`LineStripNode.colour`** (`dart fix`). `LineStripNode.colour` is `color`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="core-LodGroup"></a>**`LodGroup`** (nothing to do). Only the engine's material type in its signature changed: `Material` is `RenderMaterial` (`core-Material-RenderMaterial` renames it).
- <a id="core-LookGoal"></a>**`LookGoal`** (by hand). `AnimationGoal` has a new case, `LookGoal`: a `switch` over a `AnimationGoal` that names every case needs one for it, or a wildcard.
- <a id="rc-core-LookSettings-grade"></a>**`LookSettings`** (by hand). `lift`, `gamma` and `gain` are `LinearColor`s, one value per channel in red, green and blue, so the settings can be `const`: `lift: Vector3(0, 0, 0.1)` is `lift: LinearColor(0, 0, 0.1)`, the same numbers, and a read of `.x` is `.r`.
- <a id="core-Material"></a>**`Material`** (`dart fix`). `Material` is `RenderMaterial`. The engine's material is `RenderMaterial`, so it no longer collides with Flutter's `Material` widget and the `hide Material` clauses go. (`SurfaceMaterial` was already the format-neutral material a decoder produces.)
- <a id="core-MaterialBindings"></a>**`MaterialBindings`** (nothing to do). `usesLightList` became a final field instead of a getter. Reading it is the same; `MaterialBindings` is final, so nothing overrode it.
- <a id="f2-core-MaterialExtensions-colours"></a>**`MaterialExtensions`** (by hand). `specularColor`, `sheenColor` and `attenuationColor` are linear `LinearColor`s with `const` defaults: `Vector3(r, g, b)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="core-MaterialOutput"></a>**`MaterialOutput`** (by hand). `MaterialStatement` has a new case, `MaterialOutput` (`out name = value;`), which only a version 2 `vertex` block holds: a `switch` over a `MaterialStatement` that names every case needs one for it, or a wildcard. A reader of fragment, light, ambient or composite bodies never meets it and can refuse it; `evaluateMaterialVertex` runs a vertex block's outputs, and `emitMaterialVertex` writes them.
- <a id="core-MaterialSortIds"></a>**`MaterialSortIds`** (nothing to do). Only the engine's material type in its signature changed: `Material` is `RenderMaterial` (`core-Material-RenderMaterial` renames it).
- <a id="core-MaterialSyntaxError"></a>**`MaterialSyntaxError`** (`dart fix`). `MaterialSyntaxError` is `MaterialSyntaxException`. `*Error` is for programmer mistakes, which a caller does not catch; this is something a correct program meets, so it is an exception and is named like one. `MaterialSyntaxException` has the same constructor and members and extends `Flutter3dFormatException`.
- <a id="rc-core-MeshBuilder-addVertex"></a>**`MeshBuilder.addVertex`** (by hand). `addVertex(color:)` takes a linear `LinearColor`, as a vertex colour is: `color: Vector4(r, g, b, a)` is `color: LinearColor(r, g, b, a)`, the same numbers.
- <a id="rc-core-MeshData-withColor"></a>**`MeshData.withColor`** (by hand). `withColor` takes a linear `LinearColor`: `withColor(Vector4(r, g, b, a))` is `withColor(LinearColor(r, g, b, a))`, and `withColor(linearFromSrgb(r, g, b))` is `withColor(LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b))`.
- <a id="core-MeshGeometry"></a>**`MeshGeometry`** (`migrate`). `MeshGeometry` is a base mixin class: `implements MeshGeometry` becomes `with MeshGeometry`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="core-MeshNode"></a>**`MeshNode`** (nothing to do). Only the engine's material type in its signature changed: `Material` is `RenderMaterial` (`core-Material-RenderMaterial` renames it).
- <a id="f2-core-MeshNode-outlineColor"></a>**`MeshNode.outlineColor`** (by hand). `MeshNode.outlineColor` is a linear `LinearColor`. It held the display-referred channels a `Color` gives; say those as `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b)` (or `color.toLinear()`), and the ring is the colour it was.
- <a id="f2-core-MeshNode-tint"></a>**`MeshNode.tint`** (by hand). `MeshNode.tint` is a `LinearColor` assigned whole: `node.tint.setValues(1, 0.3, 0.3, 1)` is `node.tint = LinearColor(1, 0.3, 0.3)`, `tint.w` is `tint.a`.
- <a id="4a-core-MeshNode-worldBoundsCentre"></a>**`MeshNode.worldBoundsCentre`** (`dart fix`). `MeshNode.worldBoundsCentre` is `worldBoundsCenter`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-MeshNode-worldIsMirrored"></a>**`MeshNode.worldIsMirrored`** (`dart fix`). `MeshNode.worldIsMirrored` is `isWorldMirrored`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="rc-core-MeshOverlay-colours"></a>**`MeshOverlay`** (by hand). `edge`, `point`, `ribbon` and `wash` take a linear `LinearColor` and write it as it is; they took the design's sRGB numbers in a `Vector4` and decoded them with `asDrawn`, which is gone. The same picture is `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a)` of the old numbers, and `MeshOverlay.asDrawn(v)` is `LinearColor.fromSrgb(v.x, v.y, v.z, v.w)`.
- <a id="4a-core-MeshOverlay-asDrawn"></a>**`MeshOverlay.asDrawn`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-MeshOverlay-edge"></a>**`MeshOverlay.edge`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-MeshOverlay-point"></a>**`MeshOverlay.point`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-MeshOverlay-ribbon"></a>**`MeshOverlay.ribbon`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="4a-core-MeshOverlay-wash"></a>**`MeshOverlay.wash`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="f3-core-ModelDecoder-base"></a>**`ModelDecoder`** (by hand). `ModelDecoder` is an `abstract base class`, so a member added in a minor comes with a default and breaks no decoder. A decoder of your own that wrote `implements ModelDecoder` now writes `extends ModelDecoder` and is `final` or `base` itself; its members stay as they were, and a `const` constructor still works.
- <a id="f3-core-ModelDocument-base"></a>**`ModelDocument`** (by hand). `ModelDocument` is an `abstract base class`. A document of your own already extended it; the class that extends it is now `final` or `base` itself (`final class MyDocument extends ModelDocument`).
- <a id="f3-core-ModelFormat-class"></a>**`ModelFormat`** (by hand). `ModelFormat` is a class with constant values instead of an enum, so a later release can name a format without breaking a `switch` written against this one. `ModelFormat.gltf` and the rest, `values` and `name` are unchanged; an exhaustive `switch` over it needs a default case.
- <a id="4a-core-ModelImpostor-ModelImpostor"></a>**`ModelImpostor.ModelImpostor`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-ModelImpostor-centre"></a>**`ModelImpostor.centre`** (`dart fix`). `ModelImpostor.centre` is `center`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="f2-core-ModelLight"></a>**`ModelLight`** (by hand). `ModelLight(color:)` and `ModelLight.color` are a `LinearColor`, in linear light, where they were a `Vector3`: write `LinearColor(r, g, b)` for the same numbers.
- <a id="f2-core-ModelLight-color"></a>**`ModelLight.color`** (by hand). `ModelLight.color` is a linear `LinearColor`; `Vector3(r, g, b)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b)`, and it goes onto a `LightNode.color` as it is.
- <a id="core-ModelPart"></a>**`ModelPart`** (nothing to do). Only the engine's material type in its signature changed: `Material` is `RenderMaterial` (`core-Material-RenderMaterial` renames it).
- <a id="4a-core-ModelSplat-ModelSplat"></a>**`ModelSplat.ModelSplat`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-ModelSplat-colourSpace"></a>**`ModelSplat.colourSpace`** (`dart fix`). `ModelSplat.colourSpace` is `colorSpace`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="f3-core-ModelWriter-base"></a>**`ModelWriter`** (by hand). `ModelWriter` is an `abstract base class`. A writer of your own that wrote `implements ModelWriter` now writes `extends ModelWriter` and is `final` or `base` itself; its members stay as they were.
- <a id="core-MorphSink"></a>**`MorphSink`** (`migrate`). `MorphSink` is a base mixin class: `implements MorphSink` becomes `with MorphSink`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="core-NodeFrame"></a>**`NodeFrame`** (`dart fix`). `NodeFrame` is `RenderFrame`. What a node is handed is `RenderFrame`, a `FrameContext` that also says the view, its matrices, the jitter, the time and the frame index.
- <a id="core-OcclusionMode"></a>**`OcclusionMode`** (by hand). An open set since 1.0: a `switch` over it needs a `_ =>` case. `values`, `name`, `index` and `byName` are there.
- <a id="core-OcclusionTest"></a>**`OcclusionTest`** (`migrate`). `OcclusionTest` is a base mixin class: `implements OcclusionTest` becomes `with OcclusionTest`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="4a-core-OffAxisProjection-OffAxisProjection-symmetric"></a>**`OffAxisProjection.OffAxisProjection.symmetric`** (`dart fix`). Units in names: a field of view is `fovY`, the vertical angle, in radians (docs/CONTRACTS.md), and time in the simulation is a `double` of seconds. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-OrbitController-OrbitController"></a>**`OrbitController.OrbitController`** (`dart fix`). Units in names: a field of view is `fovY`, the vertical angle, in radians (docs/CONTRACTS.md), and time in the simulation is a `double` of seconds. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-OrbitController-frameBounds"></a>**`OrbitController.frameBounds`** (`dart fix`). Units in names: a field of view is `fovY`, the vertical angle, in radians (docs/CONTRACTS.md), and time in the simulation is a `double` of seconds. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-OrbitController-framingFov"></a>**`OrbitController.framingFov`** (`dart fix`). `OrbitController.framingFov` is `framingFovY`. Units in names: a field of view is `fovY`, the vertical angle, in radians (docs/CONTRACTS.md), and time in the simulation is a `double` of seconds. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="core-OutputTransform"></a>**`OutputTransform`** (by hand). An open set since 1.0: a `switch` over it needs a `_ =>` case. `values`, `name`, `index` and `byName` are there.
- <a id="4a-core-OverlayBatch-vertex"></a>**`OverlayBatch.vertex`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="rc-core-OverlayBatch-vertex"></a>**`OverlayBatch.vertex`** (by hand). `vertex(at, color)` takes the linear `LinearColor` it writes: `Vector4(r, g, b, a)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b, a)`, the same numbers.
- <a id="core-PackedLane"></a>**`PackedLane`** (`dart fix`). `PackedLane` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-PackedMap"></a>**`PackedMap`** (`dart fix`). `PackedMap` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-PassSkip"></a>**`PassSkip`** (by hand). `PassSkip` has a new value, `switchedOff`, for a pass its settings turned off: a `switch` over a `PassSkip` that names every value needs a case for it, or a wildcard.
- <a id="4a-core-PerspectiveProjection-PerspectiveProjection"></a>**`PerspectiveProjection.PerspectiveProjection`** (`dart fix`). Units in names: a field of view is `fovY`, the vertical angle, in radians (docs/CONTRACTS.md), and time in the simulation is a `double` of seconds. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-PerspectiveProjection-copyWith"></a>**`PerspectiveProjection.copyWith`** (`dart fix`). Units in names: a field of view is `fovY`, the vertical angle, in radians (docs/CONTRACTS.md), and time in the simulation is a `double` of seconds. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-PerspectiveProjection-fovYRadians"></a>**`PerspectiveProjection.fovYRadians`** (`dart fix`). `PerspectiveProjection.fovYRadians` is `fovY`. Units in names: a field of view is `fovY`, the vertical angle, in radians (docs/CONTRACTS.md), and time in the simulation is a `double` of seconds. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="rc-core-PhotoFilter-grade"></a>**`PhotoFilter`** (by hand). `lift` and `gain` are `LinearColor`s, as `LookSettings`' are: `Vector3(r, g, b)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="core-Photometric"></a>**`Photometric`** (by hand). `LightNode.intensity` is already lux or candela since 1.0, so `fromLux`, `fromCandela`, `toLux` and `toCandela` went: write the number. Before 1.0 they converted at 800 lm per engine unit; to keep a scene that used them as it looked, multiply by `Photometric.legacyUnit / Photometric.bulbAtOneMeter` (about 91). `referenceIlluminance` is `Photometric.bulbAtOneMeter`.
- <a id="f2-core-Photometric-toEngine"></a>**`Photometric.toEngine`** (by hand). The renderer's own light scale is not API since 1.0: everything takes lux, candela or nits and the renderer converts. To carry a pre-1.0 number over, multiply it by `Photometric.legacyUnit` (lights, ambient, the sky's sun) or `Photometric.legacyNits` (emissive strength).
- <a id="f2-core-PhysicalCamera-luxPerUnit"></a>**`PhysicalCamera.luxPerUnit`** (`migrate`). `PhysicalCamera.luxPerUnit` becomes `Photometric.legacyUnit`. The renderer's scale is internal; the number it was, about 5 790.6, is `Photometric.legacyUnit`.
- <a id="f2-core-PhysicalCamera-nitsPerUnit"></a>**`PhysicalCamera.nitsPerUnit`** (`migrate`). `PhysicalCamera.nitsPerUnit` becomes `Photometric.legacyNits`. The renderer's scale is internal; the number it was, 1 843.2, is `Photometric.legacyNits`, for carrying a pre-1.0 emissive over.
- <a id="f2-core-PlanarReflectorNode-tint"></a>**`PlanarReflectorNode.tint`** (by hand). `tint` is a linear `LinearColor`, settable now: `tint: Vector3(r, g, b)` is `tint: LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="core-PointerTargets"></a>**`PointerTargets`** (nothing to do). Only the engine's material type in its signature changed: `Material` is `RenderMaterial` (`core-Material-RenderMaterial` renames it).
- <a id="core-Pose"></a>**`Pose`** (`dart fix`). `Pose` is `AnimationPose`. The engine's animation pose is `AnimationPose`, so it no longer shares a name with the simulation's `Pose`.
- <a id="core-ProbeFace"></a>**`ProbeFace`** (`dart fix`). `ProbeFace` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-Ray"></a>**`Ray`** (`dart fix`). `Ray` is `LocalRay`. The geometry library's float32 ray is `LocalRay`, so it no longer collides with vector_math's `Ray`. A ray in the world, with a double-precision origin, is the new `WorldRay`.
- <a id="core-Raycaster"></a>**`Raycaster`** (nothing to do). Only the float32 ray in its signature changed: `Ray` is `LocalRay` (`core-Ray-LocalRay` renames it).
- <a id="core-ReachGoal"></a>**`ReachGoal`** (by hand). `AnimationGoal` has a new case, `ReachGoal`: a `switch` over a `AnimationGoal` that names every case needs one for it, or a wildcard.
- <a id="core-ReactiveFrame"></a>**`ReactiveFrame`** (nothing to do). A `ReactiveFrame` was only ever made by the renderer, which still makes it; the constructor took one of the engine's own uniform blocks.
- <a id="f2-core-RenderMaterial-baseColor"></a>**`RenderMaterial.baseColor`** (by hand). `RenderMaterial.baseColor` is a `LinearColor`, linear, and assigned whole. It was a `Vector4` holding the sRGB-encoded colour and changed in place. `baseColor: Vector4(r, g, b, a)` is `baseColor: LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a)` (the picture does not change); `material.baseColor.setValues(...)` is `material.baseColor = LinearColor.fromSrgb(...)`; a read of `.x` that wanted the encoded number is `.toSrgb().r`, and `.w` is `.a`.
- <a id="f2-core-RenderMaterial-emissive"></a>**`RenderMaterial.emissive`** (by hand). `RenderMaterial.emissive` is a linear `LinearColor`, assigned whole: `emissive: Vector3(r, g, b)` is `emissive: LinearColor(r, g, b)`, and `emissive.setValues(r, g, b)` is `emissive = LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="f2-core-RenderMaterial-emissiveStrength"></a>**`RenderMaterial.emissiveStrength`** (by hand). `emissiveStrength` is nits (cd/m²) since 1.0, the luminance an emissive of white shines at; the default is `Photometric.legacyNits` (1 843.2), which is what the old default of one drew. Multiply a pre-1.0 number by `Photometric.legacyNits` to keep the glow: `emissiveStrength: 4.0` is `4.0 * Photometric.legacyNits`. A `SurfaceMaterial`'s strength is a file's plain multiple and is unchanged; loading converts it.
- <a id="core-RenderNode"></a>**`RenderNode`** (by hand). `preferredPhase` is `defaultAnchor`: return `RenderAnchor.beforePresent` where it returned `FramePhase.present`, `RenderAnchor.afterScene` for `FramePhase.overlay`. `execute` takes a `RenderFrame` (the renamed `NodeFrame`).
- <a id="core-RenderNodeRegistry"></a>**`RenderNodeRegistry`** (by hand). `Renderer.nodes` went with the other four ways into the frame: a pass is added with `renderer.renderSteps.addNode`, which hands back a `Registration`; `renderSteps.nodesAt(anchor)` lists what is where.
- <a id="core-RenderServices"></a>**`RenderServices`** (`migrate`). `RenderServices` is a base mixin class: `implements RenderServices` becomes `with RenderServices`, on a `base` class.
- <a id="core-RenderSettings"></a>**`RenderSettings`** (by hand). `clusteredLights` is on by default: a scene with more than eight lights is lit per cell of the view rather than per draw, and draws a different frame. Pass `clusteredLights: false` where the old picture is wanted. The physical camera is the default exposure model too (`camera`, `physicalCamera`), and its default camera draws exactly the old exposure, so `physicalCamera: false` is needed only where a camera is set and the multiplier alone is wanted.
- <a id="f2-core-RenderSettings-copyWith-disabledPasses"></a>**`RenderSettings.copyWith`** (by hand). `settings.copyWith(disabledPasses: {...})` is `settings.without({...})` over the steps that own those passes; see `f2-core-RenderSettings-disabledPasses` for the names.
- <a id="f2-core-RenderSettings-disabledPasses"></a>**`RenderSettings.disabledPasses`** (by hand). `RenderSettings(disabledPasses: {...})` is `RenderSettings(...).without({...})` over the steps that own those passes: `bloom` is `RenderStep.bloom`, `lens flare` `RenderStep.lensFlare`, `ssao` and `ssao blur` `RenderStep.ambientOcclusion`, `contact shadows` `RenderStep.contactShadows`, `reflections` `RenderStep.reflections`, `light shafts` `RenderStep.lightShafts`, `volumetric fog` `RenderStep.volumetricFog`, `depth of field` `RenderStep.depthOfField`, `motion blur` `RenderStep.motionBlur`, `luminance` `RenderStep.autoExposure`, `local exposure` `RenderStep.localExposure`, `spatial upscale` `RenderStep.spatialUpscale`, `antialias` `RenderStep.edgeSmoothing` and `RenderStep.sharpening`, `decals` `RenderStep.decals`, `depth pyramid` `RenderStep.hiZOcclusion`, `viewport shading` `RenderStep.viewportShading`, the shadow passes `RenderStep.shadows`; `RenderStep.ownsPass` answers for any other name. A pass of your own is switched off by the `RenderStep` it was added with (`renderSteps.addNode(node, step: myStep)`). A skipped pass is reported as `PassSkip.switchedOff` rather than `PassSkip.disabled`. The transparent half and the scene colour copy have no step: they run when the scene holds glass.
- <a id="core-RenderSettings-needsSurfaceBuffer"></a>**`RenderSettings.needsSurfaceBuffer`** (by hand). A setting cannot see the frame, so `needsSurfaceBuffer` only ever modelled the built-in passes. Ask the compiled frame instead: `compiled.isConsumed(FrameResourceIds.surfaceBuffer)` on the `CompiledFrameGraph` the renderer built.
- <a id="f2-core-RenderSettings-pixelAlteringPasses"></a>**`RenderSettings.pixelAlteringPasses`** (`dart fix`). `RenderSettings.pixelAlteringPasses` is `pixelAlteringSteps`. The set `forMeasurement` switches off is a `Set<RenderStep>` now, handed to `without`: `settings.without(RenderSettings.pixelAlteringSteps)`. A caller that read the pass names reads each step's `passes`.
- <a id="f2-core-RenderSettings-undisablePasses"></a>**`RenderSettings.undisablePasses`** (by hand). The names `disabledPasses` refused, `scene`, `composite` and `object ids`, are not steps, so no `without` can switch them off and there is nothing to subtract.
- <a id="core-RenderView-clearColor-parameter"></a>**`RenderView.RenderView`** (`dart fix`). `RenderView(clearColor: ...)` is `RenderView(clearColorSrgb: ...)`, the same sRGB `Vector4`; `RenderView.texture` takes it under the same name.
- <a id="core-RenderView-clearColor"></a>**`RenderView.clearColor`** (`dart fix`). `RenderView.clearColor` is `clearColorSrgb`. The clear colour is the one colour the engine takes sRGB-encoded, and since 1.0 a colour that is not linear says so in its name. Same `Vector4`, same values.
- <a id="core-Renderer-extension-points"></a>**`Renderer`** (by hand). One way into the frame since 1.0, `renderer.renderSteps`: `addContributor`, `removeContributor` and `contributors` moved there unchanged; `addNode(node, phase: ...)` is `renderSteps.addNode(node, at: anchor)` (`FramePhase.overlay` is `RenderAnchor.afterScene`, `FramePhase.present` is `RenderAnchor.beforePresent`, no `at` uses the node's `defaultAnchor`), and it hands back a `Registration` whose `cancel` is what `removeNode` was.
- <a id="core-Renderer-internals"></a>**`Renderer`** (by hand). The renderer's own stages and test hooks are not its API since 1.0, so a shader change is not a major release. A stage is looked up by name, `renderer.shaders['DebugLineVertex']!`; the `debug*` hooks are for the engine's own tests, through `package:flutter3d_core/src/engine/render/renderer.dart`.
- <a id="core-Renderer"></a>**`Renderer`** (nothing to do). `releaseTransientTargets` returns the bytes it handed back instead of nothing. A call that ignores the result is unchanged; only a tear-off stored as `void Function()` has to take the `int` or ignore it, and `Renderer` is final, so nothing overrides it.
- <a id="f2-core-Renderer-addMaterials"></a>**`Renderer.addMaterials`** (`migrate`). `Renderer.addMaterials` becomes `x.renderSteps.addMaterials({0})`. One way to add a bundle of materials: `renderer.renderSteps.addMaterials`, which a plugin already used. It returns the `Registration` that takes the library out again.
- <a id="4a-core-Renderer-kShadowedLights"></a>**`Renderer.kShadowedLights`** (`dart fix`). `Renderer.kShadowedLights` is `shadowedLights`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="f2-core-Renderer-removeMaterials"></a>**`Renderer.removeMaterials`** (by hand). Keep the `Registration` that `renderer.renderSteps.addMaterials(library)` returned and `cancel()` it; that takes the library out, as this did.
- <a id="f2-core-Renderer-shadowedLights"></a>**`Renderer.shadowedLights`** (nothing to do). A static getter now, with the same value (6), so a later minor can change the shadow atlas without a constant compiled into callers.
- <a id="4a-core-ResourceDesc-resolve"></a>**`ResourceDesc.resolve`** (nothing to do). Only a type or a positional parameter it names was renamed; no call names a positional parameter, and a type's rename carries its own fix.
- <a id="core-Scene"></a>**`Scene`** (by hand). A node joins its scene's lists when it is added (`Scene.add`); the `register*` calls are the nodes' own since 1.0. `ambientColor` is a `LinearColor`: `scene.ambientColor = const LinearColor(r, g, b)`, or `vector.toLinearColor()`. `addRenderTexture` is `addTextureView`, `renderTextures` is `textureViews`.
- <a id="core-Scene-origin"></a>**`Scene`** (nothing to do). Additions: `origin`, `shiftOrigin`, `rebaseAround`, `onOriginShift`, `toScene` and `toWorld`, for a world bigger than float32 holds. A node's transform is relative to the origin, which stays at the world origin unless something shifts it, so a scene that never shifts draws as it did.
- <a id="f2-core-Scene-ambientIntensity"></a>**`Scene.ambientIntensity`** (by hand). `Scene.ambientIntensity` is lux since 1.0, about 347 by default (the 0.06 it was). Multiply a pre-1.0 number by `Photometric.legacyUnit`: `scene.ambientIntensity = 0.3` is `scene.ambientIntensity = 0.3 * Photometric.legacyUnit`.
- <a id="core-SceneBvh"></a>**`SceneBvh`** (nothing to do). Only the float32 ray in its signature changed: `Ray` is `LocalRay` (`core-Ray-LocalRay` renames it).
- <a id="core-SceneNode"></a>**`SceneNode`** (by hand). The scene graph's change counter is the engine's own since 1.0; a world position is `node.worldPosition` (double precision) and `setWorldPosition`.
- <a id="4a-core-SceneNode-ownBoundsAreCullable"></a>**`SceneNode.ownBoundsAreCullable`** (`dart fix`). `SceneNode.ownBoundsAreCullable` is `hasCullableOwnBounds`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-SceneNode-subtreeAlwaysDrawn"></a>**`SceneNode.subtreeAlwaysDrawn`** (`dart fix`). `SceneNode.subtreeAlwaysDrawn` is `isSubtreeAlwaysDrawn`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-SceneNode-visible"></a>**`SceneNode.visible`** (`dart fix`). `SceneNode.visible` is `isVisible`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-SceneNode-visibleInHierarchy"></a>**`SceneNode.visibleInHierarchy`** (`dart fix`). `SceneNode.visibleInHierarchy` is `isVisibleInHierarchy`. A boolean getter reads as a question in 1.0: it starts with is, has, can, uses, supports, did or was, as Effective Dart asks. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="core-SceneShadows"></a>**`SceneShadows`** (`dart fix`). `SceneShadows` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-ShadowAssignment"></a>**`ShadowAssignment`** (`dart fix`). `ShadowAssignment` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-ShadowCandidate"></a>**`ShadowCandidate`** (`dart fix`). `ShadowCandidate` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="4a-core-ShadowCastingMode-drawsColour"></a>**`ShadowCastingMode.drawsColour`** (`dart fix`). `ShadowCastingMode.drawsColour` is `drawsColor`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="core-ShadowFaceScheduler"></a>**`ShadowFaceScheduler`** (`dart fix`). `ShadowFaceScheduler` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="rc-core-ShadowSettings-sunAngularRadius"></a>**`ShadowSettings.sunAngularRadius`** (nothing to do). Only the value of the constant moved, to the sun's radius of 0.2666° to three figures; it is also `SkySettings.sunAngularRadius`'s default now. Code that reads it gets the new one.
- <a id="core-ShadowSlotAllocator"></a>**`ShadowSlotAllocator`** (`dart fix`). `ShadowSlotAllocator` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="f2-core-SkyColor"></a>**`SkyColor`** (by hand). A `SkyColor` returns a linear `LinearColor` rather than a `Vector4`: `(look) => Vector4(r, g, b, 1)` is `(look) => LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="4a-core-SkyColour"></a>**`SkyColour`** (`dart fix`). `SkyColour` is `SkyColor`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="f2-core-SkyGradient-colours"></a>**`SkyGradient`** (by hand). `SkyGradient`'s stops and `sunColor` are linear `LinearColor`s, and the `SkyColor` it hands out returns a `LinearColor`.
- <a id="4a-core-SkyGradient-SkyGradient"></a>**`SkyGradient.SkyGradient`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-SkyGradient-colour"></a>**`SkyGradient.colour`** (`dart fix`). `SkyGradient.colour` is `color`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-SkyGradient-sunColour"></a>**`SkyGradient.sunColour`** (`dart fix`). `SkyGradient.sunColour` is `sunColor`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="core-SkySettings"></a>**`SkySettings`** (by hand). The sky's colours are `LinearColor`s: `zenith`, `horizon`, `nadir`, `sunColor` and `tint`, in the constructor, in `copyWith` and from the `resolved*` getters. The numbers mean what they did (linear, scene-referred), so `Vector3(0.1, 0.2, 0.5)` becomes `LinearColor(0.1, 0.2, 0.5)`, or `vector.toLinearColor()`; read `.r`, `.g`, `.b` where you read `.x`, `.y`, `.z`. `directionToSun` stays a `Vector3`, and `sample` still answers one.
- <a id="f2-core-SkySettings-sunIntensity"></a>**`SkySettings.sunIntensity`** (by hand). The disc's `sunIntensity` is lux since 1.0, as a directional light is: multiply a pre-1.0 number by `Photometric.legacyUnit`. Zero is still no disc.
- <a id="rc-core-SolidColorTexture"></a>**`SolidColorTexture`** (by hand). `SolidColorTexture(color)` takes a linear `LinearColor` and stores it sRGB-encoded, as a base-colour texture's texels are. The `Vector4` it took was the encoded texel, so the same bytes are `SolidColorTexture(LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a))`, or `SolidColorTexture.texel(r, g, b, a: a)` for a texture of data rather than colour (a normal map's). `color` is `texel`, the stored values; `white` and `flatNormal` are constants.
- <a id="4a-core-SplatCloud-SplatCloud"></a>**`SplatCloud.SplatCloud`** (`dart fix`). American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-SplatCloud-centres"></a>**`SplatCloud.centres`** (`dart fix`). `SplatCloud.centres` is `centers`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-SplatCloud-colours"></a>**`SplatCloud.colours`** (`dart fix`). `SplatCloud.colours` is `colors`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="4a-core-SplatColourSpace"></a>**`SplatColourSpace`** (`dart fix`). `SplatColourSpace` is `SplatColorSpace`. American spelling in identifiers, as Flutter and Dart use: the old British name is gone, and the new one means the same.
- <a id="core-SplatOctreeException"></a>**`SplatOctreeException`** (nothing to do). `SplatOctreeException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on SplatOctreeException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="core-SplatPlyException"></a>**`SplatPlyException`** (nothing to do). `SplatPlyException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on SplatPlyException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="core-SplatSpzException"></a>**`SplatSpzException`** (nothing to do). `SplatSpzException` is still a final class with the same members; it now extends `Flutter3dFormatException` (and through it `Flutter3dException`) instead of implementing `Exception` directly. Every `on SplatSpzException` and `on Exception` still catches it, and nothing outside the package could extend it.
- <a id="f2-core-SurfaceMaterial-colours"></a>**`SurfaceMaterial`** (by hand). `SurfaceMaterial.baseColor` and `emissive` are linear `LinearColor`s. `baseColor` held the authored sRGB-encoded tint as a `Vector4`; it is `LinearColor.fromSrgb(r, g, b, a)` of the same numbers. Files keep what they stored: each reader and writer converts.
- <a id="core-TonemapCurve-agxFull"></a>**`TonemapCurve.agxFull`** (`dart fix`). `TonemapCurve.agxFull` is `agx`. `TonemapCurve.agx` is the full AgX transform since 0.7.4; `agxFull` is the same curve under its old name.
- <a id="core-TonemapCurve-values"></a>**`TonemapCurve.values`** (nothing to do). The list no longer holds `agxFull`, which is gone; it drew the same picture as `agx`. Code that walks the list sees five curves where it saw six.
- <a id="core-TransparencyMode"></a>**`TransparencyMode`** (by hand). An open set since 1.0: a `switch` over it needs a `_ =>` case. `values`, `name`, `index` and `byName` are there.
- <a id="core-TriangleBvh"></a>**`TriangleBvh`** (nothing to do). Only the float32 ray in its signature changed: `Ray` is `LocalRay` (`core-Ray-LocalRay` renames it).
- <a id="core-TriggerCondition"></a>**`TriggerCondition`** (by hand). `AnimationCondition` has a new case, `TriggerCondition`: a `switch` over a `AnimationCondition` that names every case needs one for it, or a wildcard.
- <a id="core-TwoBoneIk"></a>**`TwoBoneIk`** (nothing to do). Only the animation pose in its signature changed: `Pose` is `AnimationPose` (`core-Pose-AnimationPose` renames it).
- <a id="core-ViewModelNode"></a>**`ViewModelNode`** (nothing to do). Only the frame type in its signature changed: `NodeFrame` is `RenderFrame` (`core-NodeFrame-RenderFrame` renames it).
- <a id="f2-core-VolumetricFogSettings-colours"></a>**`VolumetricFogSettings`** (by hand). `color` and `ambient` are linear `LinearColor`s; see `f2-core-settings-colours`.
- <a id="f2-core-XraySettings-color"></a>**`XraySettings`** (by hand). `color` is a linear `LinearColor`; see `f2-core-settings-colours`.
- <a id="core-animationTrackKey"></a>**`animationTrackKey`** (`dart fix`). `animationTrackKey` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="4a-core-averageCacheMissRatio"></a>**`averageCacheMissRatio`** (nothing to do). Only a type its declaration names was renamed; that rename's own fix carries every use.
- <a id="rc-core-buildPolyline-colours"></a>**`buildPolyline`** (by hand). `colors:` is a `List<LinearColor>` and `color:` a `LinearColor`, linear as every vertex colour is: `Vector4(r, g, b, a)` is `LinearColor(r, g, b, a)`.
- <a id="4a-core-buildPolyline"></a>**`buildPolyline`** (nothing to do). Only a type its declaration names was renamed; that rename's own fix carries every use.
- <a id="core-chooseUniversalTarget"></a>**`chooseUniversalTarget`** (`dart fix`). `chooseUniversalTarget` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-clusterEye"></a>**`clusterEye`** (`dart fix`). `clusterEye` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-decodeImagePure"></a>**`decodeImagePure`** (by hand). `decodeImagePure` completes with an `Rgba8Image` or fails with an `ImageFormatException`; it no longer completes with null. Drop the `!` and the null check, and catch the exception where an unreadable image is expected.
- <a id="core-decodeJpeg"></a>**`decodeJpeg`** (by hand). `decodeJpeg` returns a `DecodedImage` and throws an `ImageFormatException` instead of returning null; `decodePng(b) ?? decodeJpeg(b)` becomes `isPng(b) ? decodePng(b) : decodeJpeg(b)` inside a `try`.
- <a id="core-decodeOctahedral"></a>**`decodeOctahedral`** (`dart fix`). `decodeOctahedral` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-decodeOctahedral-parity"></a>**`decodeOctahedral`** (nothing to do). Still exported by `flutter3d_core.dart`; only `parity_scene.dart`, which is gone, re-exported it too.
- <a id="core-decodePng"></a>**`decodePng`** (by hand). `decodePng` returns a `DecodedImage` and throws an `ImageFormatException` saying why instead of returning null. Replace `if (decoded == null)` with a `try`/`on ImageFormatException`, or ask `isPng(bytes)` first when only the signature matters.
- <a id="core-encodeOctahedral"></a>**`encodeOctahedral`** (`dart fix`). `encodeOctahedral` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-encodeOctahedral-parity"></a>**`encodeOctahedral`** (nothing to do). Still exported by `flutter3d_core.dart`; only `parity_scene.dart`, which is gone, re-exported it too.
- <a id="core-ensureSphereCapacity"></a>**`ensureSphereCapacity`** (`dart fix`). `ensureSphereCapacity` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-escapesAssetBase"></a>**`escapesAssetBase`** (`dart fix`). `escapesAssetBase` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="f2-core-gather-sky"></a>**`gather`** (by hand). `gather(..., sky:)` and `gatherProbe(..., sky:)` take a linear `LinearColor`, black by default: `sky: Vector3(r, g, b)` is `sky: LinearColor(r, g, b)`.
- <a id="f2-core-gatherProbe-sky"></a>**`gatherProbe`** (by hand). `sky:` is a linear `LinearColor`; see `f2-core-gather-sky`.
- <a id="core-gatherProbe"></a>**`gatherProbe`** (`dart fix`). `gatherProbe` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="4a-core-impostorCard"></a>**`impostorCard`** (nothing to do). Only a type its declaration names was renamed; that rename's own fix carries every use.
- <a id="core-impostorDecode"></a>**`impostorDecode`** (`dart fix`). `impostorDecode` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-impostorEncode"></a>**`impostorEncode`** (`dart fix`). `impostorEncode` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="core-jitterOffset"></a>**`jitterOffset`** (`dart fix`). `jitterOffset` is imported from `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart`.
- <a id="4a-core-kDefaultVertexCacheSize"></a>**`kDefaultVertexCacheSize`** (`dart fix`). `kDefaultVertexCacheSize` is `defaultVertexCacheSize`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-kF3dHeaderBytes"></a>**`kF3dHeaderBytes`** (`dart fix`). `kF3dHeaderBytes` is `f3dHeaderBytes`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-kF3dMagic"></a>**`kF3dMagic`** (`dart fix`). `kF3dMagic` is `f3dMagic`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="4a-core-kF3dSectionEntryBytes"></a>**`kF3dSectionEntryBytes`** (`dart fix`). `kF3dSectionEntryBytes` is `f3dSectionEntryBytes`. A public constant is lowerCamelCase in 1.0, as Effective Dart asks, without the k prefix. The value is the same under the new name.
- <a id="core-kF3dVersion"></a>**`kF3dVersion`** (nothing to do). It is the newest `.f3d` version this build reads, now 2 (the directory flags word and per-section stride). Every version-1 file still reads, and the writer still writes version 1 unless extra sections are given.

Names re-exported from `package:flutter3d_hardware/flutter3d_hardware.dart` are imported from `package:flutter3d_hardware/flutter3d_hardware.dart`. `package:flutter3d_core/flutter3d_core.dart` no longer re-exports the graphics vocabulary. A file that names `GraphicsDevice`, `TextureFormat` or a descriptor imports `package:flutter3d_hardware/flutter3d_hardware.dart`, and the project depends on `flutter3d_hardware`. `package:flutter3d/flutter3d.dart` still names what an application spells of it.
